key: series:1/task:for-Selectivity_Amidation_BenzoicAcid_Ethylamine_Participant_NEthylbenzamide_Evidence_1_ExperimentSpecific/description
    // [hlo:SeriesSpecific] Please describe Calculating the selectivity (what was done and how - include the formula/method used)?
    // source: E: ELN AI4Green AI4-001, results[0], results[1], results[2], substances[5].unreacted_limiting_reactant_mass
Calculated from yield and conversion: S = Y / X = 81.86 % / 95 % = 86.2 %; the ELN record gives 86 %. The conversion follows from the unreacted limiting reactant, 5 mg of 100 mg benzoic acid: X = 95 %. It is not in the crate, because the record names no substance it refers to.

    // [hlo:SeriesSpecific] Please describe Calculating the yield (what was done and how - include the formula/method used)?
    // source: E: ELN AI4Green AI4-001, substances[5].mass, .theoretical_mass, results[0]
AI4Green calculates the yield from the mass of the isolated product and its theoretical mass (from the amount of the limiting reactant, benzoic acid, 0.82 mmol): Y = 100 mg / 122.17 mg = 81.86 %.

key: series:1/task:Reacting_at_room_temperature/description
    // [hlo:SeriesSpecific] Please describe Reacting at room temperature (optional, leave empty to skip)?
